        function addToBasket() {ਊ਀
            try {ਊ਀
                // Prepriਊ愁樀 猀攀Ⰰ 搀愀 樀攀 椀稀爀愀਀Ċun posodobljenਊ਀

                const qtyArr = qtyStr.split(',').map(x => parseFloat(x.trim().replace(/\./g, ''))).filter(x => !isNaN(x) && x > 0);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                const oqStr = (document.getElementById('ordered-quantity') ? document.getElementById('ordered-quantity').value : '') || "";ਊ਀
                const oqArr = oqStr.split(',').map(x => parseFloat(x.trim().replace(/\./g, ''))).filter(x => !isNaN(x) && x > 0);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                if (qtyArr.length === 0) {ਊ਀
                    alert("Prosimo vnesite vsaj eno naklado.");ਊ਀
                    return;ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                // Pripravimo podatke za vsako nakladoਊ਀

                qtyArr.forEach((q, idx) => {ਊ਀
                    const oqVal = oqArr[idx] || q;ਊ਀
                    const res = calculateForSingleQty(q, undefined, undefined, undefined, oqVal);ਊ਀
                    if (res) results.push(res);ਊ਀
                });ਊ਀
ਊ਀

ਊ                挀漀渀猀琀 瀀爀攀瀀吀椀洀攀䤀渀瀀 㴀 搀漀挀甀洀攀渀琀⸀最攀琀䔀氀攀洀攀渀琀䈀礀䤀搀⠀✀挀愀氀挀ⴀ洀愀挀栀椀渀攀ⴀ瀀爀攀瀀ⴀ琀椀洀攀✀⤀㬀਀

ਊ                ⼀⼀ 匀愀洀漀搀攀樀渀漀 渀愀猀琀愀瘀椀 昀漀爀洀愀琀 渀愀 ✀愀甀琀漀✀Ⰰ 搀愀 猀椀猀琀攀洀 猀愀洀 瀀漀椀愀ਁĊe najboljšo polo ob menjavi strojaਊ਀
                const mFormatSelect = document.getElementById('machine-format');ਊ਀
                if (mFormatSelect) {ਊ਀
                    mFormatSelect.value = 'auto';ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                if (mType === 'digital') {ਊ਀
                    const platesInp = document.getElementById('calc-plates-num');ਊ਀
                    if (platesInp) platesInp.value = "0";ਊ਀
                    const prepInp = document.getElementById('calc-prep-price');ਊ਀
                    if (prepInp) prepInp.value = "30";ਊ਀
                    const mutInp = document.getElementById('calc-mut-plates');ਊ਀
                    if (mutInp) mutInp.value = "0";ਊ਀
                    const precutPrepInp = document.getElementById('f-precut-prep');ਊ਀
                    if (precutPrepInp) precutPrepInp.value = "10";ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
            filterAvailableFormats();ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        function filterAvailableFormats() {ਊ਀
            const sw = parseFloat(document.getElementById('calc-source-w').value) || 1000;ਊ਀
            const sh = parseFloat(document.getElementById('calc-source-h').value) || 700;ਊ਀
            const select = document.getElementById('machine-format');ਊ਀
            const options = select.options;ਊ਀
            const mType = document.getElementById('calc-machine-type').value;ਊ਀
            const profile = machineProfiles[mType];ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            for (let i = 0; i < options.length; i++) {ਊ਀
                const val = options[i].value;ਊ਀
                if (val === 'auto') continue;ਊ਀
                const sheet = sheets.find(s => s.name === val);ਊ਀
                if (sheet) {ਊ਀
                    let fitsSource = (sw >= sheet.w && sh >= sheet.h) || (sw >= sheet.h && sh >= sheet.w);ਊ਀
                    let fitsMachine = true;ਊ਀
                    if (profile && profile.maxW) {ਊ਀
                        fitsMachine = (sheet.w <= profile.maxW && sheet.h <= profile.maxH) || (sheet.h <= profile.maxW && sheet.w <= profile.maxH);ਊ਀
                    }ਊ਀
                    const fits = fitsSource && fitsMachine;ਊ਀
                    options[i].disabled = !fits;ਊ਀
                    if (!fits && select.value === val) select.value = 'auto';ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
            updateSourceYield();ਊ਀
            calculate();ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        function updateSourceYield() {ਊ਀
            const sw = parseFloat(document.getElementById('calc-source-w').value) || 1000;ਊ਀
            const sh = parseFloat(document.getElementById('calc-source-h').value) || 700;ਊ਀
            const mFormat = document.getElementById('machine-format').value;ਊ਀
            let sheetW = 700, sheetH = 500;ਊ਀
            let sheet = sheets.find(s => s.name === mFormat);ਊ਀
            if (sheet) { sheetW = sheet.w; sheetH = sheet.h; }ਊ਀
            const y1 = Math.floor(sw / sheetW) * Math.floor(sh / sheetH);ਊ਀
            const y2 = Math.floor(sw / sheetH) * Math.floor(sh / sheetW);ਊ਀
            // If auto format, calculate will overwrite this anyway with the actual best layout's yield.ਊ਀
            if (mFormat !== 'auto') {ਊ਀
                document.getElementById('calc-source-yield').value = Math.max(y1, y2, 1);ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        function optimizeLayout(sheetW, sheetH, itemW, itemH, gripper, forceMode) {ਊ਀
            const isObrat = document.getElementById('calc-is-obrat') ? document.getElementById('calc-is-obrat').checked : false;ਊ਀
            let best = null;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            const baseConfigs = [ਊ਀
                { iw: itemW, ih: itemH, rot: false, edge: 'H' },ਊ਀
                { iw: itemH, ih: itemW, rot: true, edge: 'H' },ਊ਀
                { iw: itemW, ih: itemH, rot: false, edge: 'W' },ਊ਀
                { iw: itemH, ih: itemW, rot: true, edge: 'W' }ਊ਀
            ];ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            baseConfigs.forEach(cfg => {ਊ਀
                let usableW = cfg.edge === 'W' ? sheetW - gripper : sheetW;ਊ਀
                let usableH = cfg.edge === 'H' ? sheetH - gripper : sheetH;ਊ਀
                if (usableW <= 0 || usableH <= 0) return;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                let cols = Math.floor(usableW / cfg.iw);ਊ਀
                let rows = Math.floor(usableH / cfg.ih);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                let isPlacedLandscape = cfg.iw >= cfg.ih;ਊ਀
                if (forceMode === 'landscape' && !isPlacedLandscape) return;ਊ਀
                if (forceMode === 'portrait' && isPlacedLandscape && cfg.iw !== cfg.ih) return;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                let count = cols * rows;ਊ਀
                let finalCount = count;ਊ਀
                let isMixed = false;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                if (forceMode === 'auto' && !isObrat && count > 0) {ਊ਀
                    // Odstranjena avtomatska "mešana" postavitev (isMixed), da se ohrani simetrija in enotna smer vlaken.ਊ਀
                    // Če stranka želi dodati veਊ 猀琀愀瘀欀漀瘀Ⰰ 猀攀 洀漀爀愀 琀漀 爀漀਀Ċno preveriti, sicer sistem vsiljuje nesimetriਊ渁攀 瀀漀氀攀⸀਀

            let totalSourceSheetsNeeded = Math.ceil(totalSheetsNeeded / sourceYield);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            if (paperUnit === 'ton') {ਊ਀
                let areaM2 = (sourceW / 1000) * (sourceH / 1000);ਊ਀
                let weightKg = areaM2 * (paperWeight / 1000) * totalSourceSheetsNeeded;ਊ਀
                paperCost = (weightKg / 1000) * paperPrice;ਊ਀
            } else if (paperUnit === '1000') {ਊ਀
                // Cena na 1000 osnovnih polਊ਀
                paperCost = (totalSourceSheetsNeeded / 1000) * paperPrice;ਊ਀
            } else {ਊ਀
                paperCost = totalSourceSheetsNeeded * paperPrice;ਊ਀

            function getDynamicSpeed(baseSpeed, grammage, qty) {ਊ਀
                let _front = parseInt(document.getElementById('calc-color-front').value) || 0;ਊ਀
                let _back = parseInt(document.getElementById('calc-color-back').value) || 0;ਊ਀
                let _isObrat = document.getElementById('calc-is-obrat').checked;ਊ਀
                let mutPlatesVal = parseInt(document.getElementById('calc-mut-plates').value) || 0;ਊ਀
                let mutSuffix = "";ਊ਀
                if (mutPlatesVal === 1) mutSuffix = " + mutacija 1x";ਊ਀
                else if (mutPlatesVal === 2) mutSuffix = " + mutacija 2x";ਊ਀
                else if (mutPlatesVal >= 3) mutSuffix = " + mutacija 3x";ਊ਀
                let baseColorMode = _isObrat ? '4/OB' : (_back > 0 ? '4/4' : '4/0');ਊ਀
                let speedTableMode = baseColorMode + mutSuffix;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                let bossTable = getBossTables()[speedTableMode] || getBossTables()[baseColorMode];ਊ਀
                if (bossTable) {ਊ਀
                    let getS = (obj) => {ਊ਀
                        if (paperWeight <= 150) return obj.s150;ਊ਀
                        if (paperWeight <= 250) {ਊ਀
                            let f = (paperWeight - 150) / 100;ਊ਀
                            return obj.s150 + f * (obj.s250 - obj.s150);ਊ਀
                        }ਊ਀
                        if (paperWeight <= 350) {ਊ਀
                            let f = (paperWeight - 250) / 100;ਊ਀
                            return obj.s250 + f * (obj.s350 - obj.s250);ਊ਀
                        }ਊ਀
                        return obj.s350;ਊ਀
                    };ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                    let s = getS(bossTable[0]);ਊ਀
                    if (qty <= bossTable[0].q) {ਊ਀
                        s = getS(bossTable[0]);ਊ਀
                    } else if (qty >= bossTable[bossTable.length - 1].q) {ਊ਀
                        s = getS(bossTable[bossTable.length - 1]);ਊ਀
                    } else {ਊ਀
                        for (let i = 0; i < bossTable.length - 1; i++) {ਊ਀
                            if (qty >= bossTable[i].q && qty <= bossTable[i + 1].q) {ਊ਀
                                let rangeQ = bossTable[i + 1].q - bossTable[i].q;ਊ਀
                                let speed1 = getS(bossTable[i]);ਊ਀
                                let speed2 = getS(bossTable[i + 1]);ਊ਀
                                let rangeS = speed2 - speed1;ਊ਀
                                let fraction = (qty - bossTable[i].q) / rangeQ;ਊ਀
                                s = speed1 + fraction * rangeS;ਊ਀
                                break;ਊ਀
                            }ਊ਀
                        }ਊ਀
                    }ਊ਀
                    return Math.round(s);ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                return Math.min(baseSpeed, 6100);ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            let frontRaw = parseInt(document.getElementById('calc-color-front').value);ਊ਀
            let front = isNaN(frontRaw) ? 0 : frontRaw;ਊ਀
            let backRaw = parseInt(document.getElementById('calc-color-back').value);ਊ਀

ਊ਀

            let prepPrice = parseFloat(document.getElementById('calc-prep-price').value) || 0;ਊ਀
            let changePrice = parseFloat(document.getElementById('calc-change-price').value) || 0;ਊ਀

ਊ਀

            let totalPrepCost = (platesNum * platesPrice) + prepPrice + (numberOfChanges * changePrice) + washCost;ਊ਀

            let mRate = parseFloat(document.getElementById('calc-machine-rate').value) || 0;ਊ਀

                let uWork = (totalSheetsNeeded / 1000) * uP1000;ਊ਀

                    name: 'UV lak (tuja)',ਊ਀

                let pSheets = sheetsNeeded * pMultiplier;ਊ਀
                let pPrice = (parseFloat(document.getElementById('f-personalization-price').value) || 0);ਊ਀
                let pCost = pSheets * pPrice;ਊ਀
                let breakdownText = `Delo: ${pSheets} pol po ${formatPrice(pPrice)}/polo = ${formatPrice(pCost)}`;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                let extraCost = 0;ਊ਀
                if (mType === 'S8') {ਊ਀
                    let autoCutSpeed = 7400;ਊ਀
                    if (paperWeight >= 300) autoCutSpeed = 4100;ਊ਀
                    else if (paperWeight > 150) autoCutSpeed = 7400 - ((paperWeight - 150) / 150) * (7400 - 4100);ਊ਀
                    let cutCost = (totalSheetsNeeded / autoCutSpeed) * 30.00;ਊ਀
                    extraCost = cutCost;ਊ਀
                    breakdownText += ` | Dodaten razrez na pol: ${formatPrice(cutCost)}`;ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
ਊ਀

                    name: 'Personalizacija' + (mType === 'S8' ? ' (Digitalni format - S8)' : ''),ਊ਀
                    cost: pCost + extraCost,ਊ਀
                    breakdown: breakdownTextਊ਀
                });ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀

                    if (cnt && (cnt.value === '0' || cnt.value === '')) {ਊ਀
                        cnt.value = '1';ਊ਀
                        updateInputStyles(cnt);ਊ਀
                    }ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
            } else {ਊ਀
                row.classList.remove('active');ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
            calculatePrice();ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        function drawAllLayouts(sheetW, sheetH, layout, gripper, origW, origH, bleed, sourceW, sourceH, isOverride = false, currentCount = 0) {ਊ਀
            g_lastSheetW = sheetW;ਊ਀
            g_lastSheetH = sheetH;ਊ਀
            g_lastG = gripper;ਊ਀
            document.getElementById('source-layout-container').style.display = 'block';ਊ਀
            document.getElementById('machine-layout-container').style.display = 'block';ਊ਀
            document.getElementById('source-dim-label').innerText = `${sourceW} x ${sourceH} mm`;ਊ਀
            document.getElementById('machine-dim-label').innerText = `${sheetW} x ${sheetH} mm`;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            drawSourceCanvas(sourceW, sourceH, sheetW, sheetH);ਊ਀
            drawMachineCanvas(sheetW, sheetH, layout, gripper, origW, origH, bleed, isOverride, currentCount);ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        function drawSourceCanvas(sw, sh, mw, mh) {ਊ਀
            const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas-source');ਊ਀
            if (!canvas) return;ਊ਀
            const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Calculate best fit (source yield)ਊ਀
            const y1 = Math.floor(sw / mw) * Math.floor(sh / mh);ਊ਀
            const y2 = Math.floor(sw / mh) * Math.floor(sh / mw);ਊ਀
            const useRotated = y2 > y1;ਊ਀
            const cols = useRotated ? Math.floor(sw / mh) : Math.floor(sw / mw);ਊ਀
            const rows = useRotated ? Math.floor(sh / mw) : Math.floor(sh / mh);ਊ਀
            const itemW = useRotated ? mh : mw;ਊ਀
            const itemH = useRotated ? mw : mh;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            const maxW = 400;ਊ਀
            const maxH = 250;ਊ਀
            const scale = Math.min(maxW / sw, maxH / sh);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            canvas.width = sw * scale;ਊ਀
            canvas.height = sh * scale;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Backgroundਊ਀
            ctx.fillStyle = '#1e293b';ਊ਀
            ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);ਊ਀
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#475569';ਊ਀
            ctx.lineWidth = 2;ਊ਀
            ctx.strokeRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Draw machine pulses insideਊ਀
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.2)';ਊ਀
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#3b82f6';ਊ਀
            ctx.lineWidth = 1;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            const startX = (sw - cols * itemW) / 2;ਊ਀
            const startY = (sh - rows * itemH) / 2;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) {ਊ਀
                for (let c = 0; c < cols; c++) {ਊ਀
                    ctx.fillRect((startX + c * itemW) * scale, (startY + r * itemH) * scale, itemW * scale, itemH * scale);ਊ਀
                    ctx.strokeRect((startX + c * itemW) * scale, (startY + r * itemH) * scale, itemW * scale, itemH * scale);ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Labelsਊ਀
            ctx.fillStyle = '#94a3b8';ਊ਀
            ctx.font = '10px Inter';ਊ਀
            ctx.fillText(`${sw} mm`, canvas.width / 2 - 15, canvas.height - 5);ਊ਀
            ctx.save();ਊ਀
            ctx.translate(10, canvas.height / 2 + 15);ਊ਀
            ctx.rotate(-Math.PI / 2);ਊ਀
            ctx.fillText(`${sh} mm`, 0, 0);ਊ਀
            ctx.restore();ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Grain direction indicator (always in direction of height 'sh')ਊ਀
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#ef4444';ਊ਀
            ctx.setLineDash([8, 4]);ਊ਀
            ctx.lineWidth = 2;ਊ਀
            const grainX = canvas.width - 15;ਊ਀
            ctx.beginPath();ਊ਀
            ctx.moveTo(grainX, 20);ਊ਀
            ctx.lineTo(grainX, canvas.height - 20);ਊ਀
            ctx.stroke();ਊ਀
            ctx.setLineDash([]);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Arrowheads for grain directionਊ਀
            ctx.fillStyle = '#ef4444';ਊ਀
            // Top arrowਊ਀
            ctx.beginPath();ਊ਀
            ctx.moveTo(grainX, 10);ਊ਀
            ctx.lineTo(grainX - 4, 20);ਊ਀
            ctx.lineTo(grainX + 4, 20);ਊ਀
            ctx.fill();ਊ਀
            // Bottom arrowਊ਀
            ctx.beginPath();ਊ਀
            ctx.moveTo(grainX, canvas.height - 10);ਊ਀
            ctx.lineTo(grainX - 4, canvas.height - 20);ਊ਀
            ctx.lineTo(grainX + 4, canvas.height - 20);ਊ਀
            ctx.fill();ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Label for grain directionਊ਀
            ctx.save();ਊ਀
            ctx.translate(grainX - 5, canvas.height / 3);ਊ਀
            ctx.rotate(-Math.PI / 2);ਊ਀
            ctx.textAlign = 'center';ਊ਀
            ctx.font = 'bold 9px Inter';ਊ਀
            ctx.fillText('SMER VLAKEN', 0, 0);ਊ਀
            ctx.restore();ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Yield labelਊ਀
            ctx.fillStyle = '#34d399';ਊ਀
            ctx.font = 'bold 12px Inter';ਊ਀
            ctx.fillText(`Izkoristek: ${cols * rows}x`, 10, 20);ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        function drawMachineCanvas(sheetW, sheetH, layout, gripper, origW, origH, bleed, isOverride = false, currentCount = 0) {ਊ਀
            const canvas = document.getElementById('canvas-machine');ਊ਀
            if (!canvas) return;ਊ਀
            const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            const maxW = 600;ਊ਀
            const maxH = 400;ਊ਀
            const scale = Math.min(maxW / sheetW, maxH / sheetH);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            canvas.width = sheetW * scale;ਊ਀
            canvas.height = sheetH * scale;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // 1. Draw Printing Sheetਊ਀
            ctx.fillStyle = '#f8fafc';ਊ਀
            ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);ਊ਀
            ctx.strokeStyle = '#475569';ਊ਀
            ctx.lineWidth = 2;ਊ਀
            ctx.strokeRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Gripperਊ਀
            ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(239, 68, 68, 0.15)';ਊ਀
            ctx.font = 'bold 10px Inter, sans-serif';ਊ਀
            if (layout && layout.gripEdge === 'W') {ਊ਀
                ctx.fillRect(0, 0, gripper * scale, canvas.height);ਊ਀
                ctx.strokeStyle = '#f43f5e';ਊ਀
                ctx.beginPath();ਊ਀
                ctx.moveTo(gripper * scale, 0);ਊ਀
                ctx.lineTo(gripper * scale, canvas.height);ਊ਀
                ctx.stroke();ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                // Label for W gripperਊ਀
                ctx.save();ਊ਀
                ctx.fillStyle = '#f43f5e';ਊ਀
                ctx.translate(10, canvas.height / 2);ਊ਀
                ctx.rotate(-Math.PI / 2);ਊ਀
                ctx.textAlign = 'center';ਊ਀
                ctx.fillText(`Prijemalec: ${gripper} mm`, 0, 0);ਊ਀
                ctx.restore();ਊ਀
            } else {ਊ਀
                ctx.fillRect(0, (canvas.height - gripper * scale), canvas.width, gripper * scale);ਊ਀
                ctx.strokeStyle = '#f43f5e';ਊ਀
                ctx.beginPath();ਊ਀
                ctx.moveTo(0, canvas.height - gripper * scale);ਊ਀
                ctx.lineTo(canvas.width, canvas.height - gripper * scale);ਊ਀
                ctx.stroke();ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                // Label for H gripperਊ਀
                ctx.fillStyle = '#f43f5e';ਊ਀
                ctx.textAlign = 'center';ਊ਀
                ctx.fillText(`Prijemalec: ${gripper} mm`, canvas.width / 2, canvas.height - (gripper * scale) / 2 + 4);ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Sheet dimension labels (Moved up so they always draw)ਊ਀
            ctx.fillStyle = '#64748b';ਊ਀
            ctx.font = '11px Inter';ਊ਀
            ctx.fillText(`${sheetW} mm`, canvas.width / 2 - 20, canvas.height - 10);ਊ਀
            ctx.save();ਊ਀
            ctx.translate(15, canvas.height / 2 + 20);ਊ਀
            ctx.rotate(-Math.PI / 2);ਊ਀
            ctx.fillText(`${sheetH} mm`, 0, 0);ਊ਀
            ctx.restore();ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Grain direction indicator - HIGH VISIBILITY BADGEਊ਀
            const sw_v = parseFloat(document.getElementById('calc-source-w').value) || 1000;ਊ਀
            const sh_v = parseFloat(document.getElementById('calc-source-h').value) || 700;ਊ਀
            const y1_v = Math.floor(sw_v / sheetW) * Math.floor(sh_v / sheetH);ਊ਀
            const y2_v = Math.floor(sw_v / sheetH) * Math.floor(sh_v / sheetW);ਊ਀
            const isGrainV = y1_v >= y2_v;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            ctx.save();ਊ਀
            const midX = canvas.width / 3;ਊ਀
            const midY = canvas.height / 3;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            if (isGrainV) {ਊ਀
                // White shadow for contrastਊ਀
                ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)';ਊ਀
                ctx.lineWidth = 4;ਊ਀
                ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(midX, 40); ctx.lineTo(midX, canvas.height - 40); ctx.stroke();ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                // Main red dashed lineਊ਀
                ctx.strokeStyle = '#ef4444';ਊ਀
                ctx.lineWidth = 2;ਊ਀
                ctx.setLineDash([12, 6]);ਊ਀
                ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(midX, 40); ctx.lineTo(midX, canvas.height - 40); ctx.stroke();ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                // Arrow headsਊ਀
                ctx.setLineDash([]);ਊ਀
                ctx.fillStyle = '#ef4444';ਊ਀
                const drawArrV = (y, rot) => {ਊ਀
                    ctx.save();ਊ਀
                    ctx.translate(midX, y);ਊ਀
                    ctx.rotate(rot);ਊ਀
                    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, 0); ctx.lineTo(-6, 12); ctx.lineTo(6, 12); ctx.fill();ਊ਀
                    ctx.restore();ਊ਀
                };ਊ਀
                drawArrV(25, 0);ਊ਀
                drawArrV(canvas.height - 25, Math.PI);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                // Text Badgeਊ਀
                ctx.save();ਊ਀
                ctx.translate(midX, midY);ਊ਀
                ctx.rotate(-Math.PI / 2);ਊ਀
                ctx.fillStyle = 'white';ਊ਀
                ctx.strokeStyle = '#ef4444';ਊ਀
                ctx.lineWidth = 1;ਊ਀
                ctx.beginPath();ਊ਀
                if (ctx.roundRect) ctx.roundRect(-45, -10, 90, 20, 10); else ctx.rect(-45, -10, 90, 20);ਊ਀
                ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();ਊ਀
                ctx.fillStyle = '#ef4444';ਊ਀
                ctx.font = 'bold 10px Inter';ਊ਀
                ctx.textAlign = 'center';ਊ਀
                ctx.fillText('SMER VLAKEN', 0, 4);ਊ਀
                ctx.restore();ਊ਀
            } else {ਊ਀
                // White shadowਊ਀
                ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.8)';ਊ਀
                ctx.lineWidth = 4;ਊ਀
                ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(40, midY); ctx.lineTo(canvas.width - 40, midY); ctx.stroke();ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                // Main red dashed lineਊ਀
                ctx.strokeStyle = '#ef4444';ਊ਀
                ctx.lineWidth = 2;ਊ਀
                ctx.setLineDash([12, 6]);ਊ਀
                ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(40, midY); ctx.lineTo(canvas.width - 40, midY); ctx.stroke();ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                // Arrow headsਊ਀
                ctx.setLineDash([]);ਊ਀
                ctx.fillStyle = '#ef4444';ਊ਀
                const drawArrH = (x, rot) => {ਊ਀
                    ctx.save();ਊ਀
                    ctx.translate(x, midY);ਊ਀
                    ctx.rotate(rot);ਊ਀
                    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(0, 0); ctx.lineTo(-6, 12); ctx.lineTo(6, 12); ctx.fill();ਊ਀
                    ctx.restore();ਊ਀
                };ਊ਀
                drawArrH(25, -Math.PI / 2);ਊ਀
                drawArrH(canvas.width - 25, Math.PI / 2);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                // Text Badgeਊ਀
                ctx.save();ਊ਀
                ctx.translate(midX, midY);ਊ਀
                ctx.fillStyle = 'white';ਊ਀
                ctx.strokeStyle = '#ef4444';ਊ਀
                ctx.lineWidth = 1;ਊ਀
                ctx.beginPath();ਊ਀
                if (ctx.roundRect) ctx.roundRect(-45, -10, 90, 20, 10); else ctx.rect(-45, -10, 90, 20);ਊ਀
                ctx.fill(); ctx.stroke();ਊ਀
                ctx.fillStyle = '#ef4444';ਊ਀
                ctx.font = 'bold 10px Inter';ਊ਀
                ctx.textAlign = 'center';ਊ਀
                ctx.fillText('SMER VLAKEN', 0, 4);ਊ਀
                ctx.restore();ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
            ctx.restore();ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Prikaz opozorila za roਊ渁椀 瘀渀漀猀 ⠀渀愀洀攀猀琀漀 爀漀稀愀 戀愀爀瘀攀⤀਀
ਊ            椀昀 ⠀椀猀伀瘀攀爀爀椀搀攀⤀ 笀਀
ਊ                ⼀⼀ 䜀氀漀眀 漀戀爀漀戀愀਀
ਊ                挀琀砀⸀猀琀爀漀欀攀匀琀礀氀攀 㴀 ✀⌀㌀戀㠀㈀昀㘀✀㬀਀
ਊ                挀琀砀⸀氀椀渀攀圀椀搀琀栀 㴀 㐀㬀਀
ਊ                挀琀砀⸀猀琀爀漀欀攀刀攀挀琀⠀　Ⰰ 　Ⰰ 挀愀渀瘀愀猀⸀眀椀搀琀栀Ⰰ 挀愀渀瘀愀猀⸀栀攀椀最栀琀⤀㬀਀
ਊ਀
ਊ                ⼀⼀ 䔀氀攀最愀渀琀渀愀 稀渀愀਀Ċka (Glassmorphism effect)ਊ਀
                ctx.save();ਊ਀
                ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.9)';ਊ਀
                ctx.shadowBlur = 10;ਊ਀
                ctx.shadowColor = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.5)';ਊ਀
                const badgeWidth = 120;ਊ਀
                const badgeHeight = 24;ਊ਀
                const bx = (canvas.width - badgeWidth) / 2;ਊ਀
                const by = 10;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                // Narišemo zaobljen pravokotnikਊ਀
                ctx.beginPath();ਊ਀
                if (ctx.roundRect) {ਊ਀
                    ctx.roundRect(bx, by, badgeWidth, badgeHeight, 6);ਊ਀
                } else {ਊ਀
                    ctx.rect(bx, by, badgeWidth, badgeHeight);ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
                ctx.fill();ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                ctx.fillStyle = '#ffffff';ਊ਀
                ctx.font = 'bold 10px Inter, sans-serif';ਊ਀
                ctx.textAlign = 'center';ਊ਀
                ctx.fillText('ROČNI VNOS', canvas.width / 2, by + 15);ਊ਀
                ctx.restore();ਊ਀
                ctx.textAlign = 'left';ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            if (g_manualLayout) {ਊ਀
                g_manualLayout.items.forEach(item => {ਊ਀
                    const x = item.x * scale;ਊ਀
                    const y = item.y * scale;ਊ਀
                    const iw = item.w * scale;ਊ਀
                    const ih = item.h * scale;ਊ਀
                    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(96, 165, 250, 0.3)';ਊ਀
                    ctx.strokeStyle = '#3b82f6';ਊ਀
                    ctx.lineWidth = 1;ਊ਀
                    ctx.fillRect(x, y, iw, ih);ਊ਀
                    ctx.strokeRect(x, y, iw, ih);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                    const innerW = iw - (2 * item.bleed * scale);ਊ਀
                    const innerH = ih - (2 * item.bleed * scale);ਊ਀
                    ctx.setLineDash([2, 3]);ਊ਀
                    ctx.strokeStyle = '#1e3a8a';ਊ਀
                    ctx.strokeRect(x + (item.bleed * scale), y + (item.bleed * scale), innerW, innerH);ਊ਀
                    ctx.setLineDash([]);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                    // Add Dimensions (Centered)ਊ਀
                    ctx.fillStyle = '#1e3a8a';ਊ਀
                    ctx.font = 'bold 9px Arial';ਊ਀
                    ctx.textAlign = 'center';ਊ਀
                    ctx.textBaseline = 'middle';ਊ਀
                    const dimLabel = `${Math.round(item.w - 2 * item.bleed)}x${Math.round(item.h - 2 * item.bleed)}`;ਊ਀
                    ctx.fillText(dimLabel, x + iw / 2, y + ih / 2);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                    // Reset alignment for next itemsਊ਀
                    ctx.textAlign = 'left';ਊ਀
                    ctx.textBaseline = 'alphabetic';ਊ਀
                });ਊ਀
                return;ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            if (!layout) return;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Gridਊ਀
            const offsetX = layout.gripEdge === 'W' ? gripper : 0;ਊ਀
            const usableW = layout.gripEdge === 'W' ? sheetW - gripper : sheetW;ਊ਀
            const usableH = layout.gripEdge === 'H' ? sheetH - gripper : sheetH;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            const targetCount = currentCount || layout.count;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Poišਊ攁洀漀 渀愀樀戀漀氀樀 甀爀愀瘀渀漀琀攀縀攁渀漀 洀爀攀縀漁 ⠀挀漀氀猀⼀爀漀眀猀⤀ 稀愀 甀渀椀昀漀爀洀渀椀 搀攀氀਀
ਊ            氀攀琀 甀渀椀昀漀爀洀䌀漀甀渀琀 㴀 氀愀礀漀甀琀⸀椀猀䴀椀砀攀搀 㼀 ⠀氀愀礀漀甀琀⸀挀漀氀猀 ⨀ 氀愀礀漀甀琀⸀爀漀眀猀⤀ 㨀 琀愀爀最攀琀䌀漀甀渀琀㬀਀
ਊ            氀攀琀 戀攀猀琀䌀 㴀 䴀愀琀栀⸀洀椀渀⠀氀愀礀漀甀琀⸀挀漀氀猀Ⰰ 甀渀椀昀漀爀洀䌀漀甀渀琀⤀㬀਀

ਊ            ⼀⼀ 䴀攀愀愁渀愀 瀀漀猀琀愀瘀椀琀攀瘀 ⠀䴀椀砀攀搀⤀ ⴀ 渀愀爀椀愀椁 瀀爀攀漀猀琀愀氀攀 欀漀猀攀਀
ਊ            氀攀琀 搀爀愀眀渀匀漀䘀愀爀 㴀 猀氀漀琀猀吀漀䐀爀愀眀⸀氀攀渀最琀栀㬀਀
ਊ            椀昀 ⠀氀愀礀漀甀琀⸀椀猀䴀椀砀攀搀 ☀☀ 搀爀愀眀渀匀漀䘀愀爀 㰀 椀琀攀洀猀吀漀䐀爀愀眀 ☀☀ ℀椀猀伀戀爀愀琀䌀栀攀挀欀攀搀⤀ 笀਀
ਊ                ⼀⼀ 倀爀攀瘀攀爀椀 欀樀攀 樀攀 瘀攀਀Ċ prostora za ostale kose (desno ali spodaj)ਊ਀
                let remW = usableW - (layout.cols * layout.itemW);ਊ਀
                let remH = usableH - (layout.rows * layout.itemH);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                if (remW >= layout.itemH) { // Rotirani kosi desnoਊ਀
                    let addCols = Math.floor(remW / layout.itemH);ਊ਀
                    let addRows = Math.floor(usableH / layout.itemW);ਊ਀
                    for (let r = 0; r < addRows && drawnSoFar < itemsToDraw; r++) {ਊ਀
                        for (let c = 0; c < addCols && drawnSoFar < itemsToDraw; c++) {ਊ਀
                            const x = (startX + (layout.cols * layout.itemW) + c * layout.itemH) * scale;ਊ਀
                            const y = (startY + r * layout.itemW) * scale;ਊ਀
                            drawOneItem(ctx, x, y, layout.itemH, layout.itemW, (layout.rot ? origW : origH), (layout.rot ? origH : origW), bleed, scale);ਊ਀
                            drawnSoFar++;ਊ਀
                        }ਊ਀
                    }ਊ਀
                } else if (remH >= layout.itemW) { // Rotirani kosi spodajਊ਀
                    let addRows = Math.floor(remH / layout.itemW);ਊ਀
                    let addCols = Math.floor(usableW / layout.itemH);ਊ਀
                    for (let r = 0; r < addRows && drawnSoFar < itemsToDraw; r++) {ਊ਀
                        for (let c = 0; c < addCols && drawnSoFar < itemsToDraw; c++) {ਊ਀
                            const x = (startX + c * layout.itemH) * scale;ਊ਀
                            const y = (startY + (layout.rows * layout.itemH) + r * layout.itemW) * scale;ਊ਀
                            drawOneItem(ctx, x, y, layout.itemH, layout.itemW, (layout.rot ? origW : origH), (layout.rot ? origH : origW), bleed, scale);ਊ਀
                            drawnSoFar++;ਊ਀
                        }ਊ਀
                    }ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀

        function toggleEmailDropdown() {ਊ਀
            var list = document.getElementById('email-dropdown-list');ਊ਀
            if (list.style.display === 'block') {ਊ਀
                list.style.display = 'none';ਊ਀
            } else {ਊ਀
                renderEmailList();ਊ਀
                list.style.display = 'block';ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        function renderEmailList() {ਊ਀
            var listDiv = document.getElementById('email-dropdown-list');ਊ਀
            if (!listDiv) return;ਊ਀
            listDiv.innerHTML = "";ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            var datalist = document.getElementById('email-list');ਊ਀
            if (!datalist || datalist.options.length === 0) {ਊ਀
                listDiv.innerHTML = '<div style="padding: 10px; color: #94a3b8; font-size: 0.8rem;">Ni shranjenih e-mailov.</div>';ਊ਀
                return;ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            for (var i = 0; i < datalist.options.length; i++) {ਊ਀
                var em = datalist.options[i].value;ਊ਀
                var item = document.createElement('div');ਊ਀
                item.style.padding = "8px 12px";ਊ਀
                item.style.cursor = "pointer";ਊ਀
                item.style.borderBottom = "1px solid #334155";ਊ਀
                item.style.fontSize = "0.9rem";ਊ਀
                item.innerText = em;ਊ਀
                item.onclick = (function (emailVal) {ਊ਀
                    return function () {ਊ਀
                        document.getElementById('calc-cust-email').value = emailVal;ਊ਀
                        document.getElementById('email-dropdown-list').style.display = 'none';ਊ਀
                    };ਊ਀
                })(em);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                item.onmouseover = function () { this.style.background = "#334155"; };ਊ਀
                item.onmouseout = function () { this.style.background = "transparent"; };ਊ਀
                listDiv.appendChild(item);ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        function toggleCustomerDropdown() {ਊ਀
            var list = document.getElementById('cust-dropdown-list');ਊ਀
            if (list.style.display === 'block') {ਊ਀
                list.style.display = 'none';ਊ਀
            } else {ਊ਀
                renderCustomerList();ਊ਀
                list.style.display = 'block';ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        function renderCustomerList() {ਊ਀
            try {ਊ਀
                var raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);ਊ਀
                var arhiv = [];ਊ਀
                if (raw) arhiv = JSON.parse(raw);ਊ਀
                if (!Array.isArray(arhiv)) arhiv = [];ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                var customers = [];ਊ਀
                for (var i = 0; i < arhiv.length; i++) {ਊ਀
                    var p = arhiv[i];ਊ਀
                    if (p && p.customer) {ਊ਀
                        var c = p.customer.trim();ਊ਀
                        if (c && customers.indexOf(c) === -1) {ਊ਀
                            customers.push(c);ਊ਀
                        }ਊ਀
                    }ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
                customers.sort();ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                var listDiv = document.getElementById('cust-dropdown-list');ਊ਀
                if (!listDiv) return;ਊ਀
                listDiv.innerHTML = "";ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                if (customers.length === 0) {ਊ਀
                    listDiv.innerHTML = '<div style="padding: 10px; color: #94a3b8; font-size: 0.8rem;">Arhiv strank je prazen.</div>';ਊ਀
                    return;ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                for (var j = 0; j < customers.length; j++) {ਊ਀
                    var item = document.createElement('div');ਊ਀
                    item.style.padding = "8px 12px";ਊ਀
                    item.style.cursor = "pointer";ਊ਀
                    item.style.borderBottom = "1px solid #334155";ਊ਀
                    item.style.fontSize = "0.9rem";ਊ਀
                    item.innerText = customers[j];ਊ਀
                    item.onclick = (function (name) {ਊ਀
                        return function () {ਊ਀
                            document.getElementById('calc-customer').value = name;ਊ਀
                            document.getElementById('cust-dropdown-list').style.display = 'none';ਊ਀
                            handleCustomerUpdate(name);ਊ਀
                        };ਊ਀
                    })(customers[j]);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                    item.onmouseover = function () { this.style.background = "#334155"; };ਊ਀
                    item.onmouseout = function () { this.style.background = "transparent"; };ਊ਀
                    listDiv.appendChild(item);ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
            } catch (e) { alert("Exception in " + "Function" + ": " + e.message + "\n" + e.stack); logDebug("Napaka renderCustomerList: " + e.message, true); }ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀

                        minusPrice: gc('calc-minus-price'),ਊ਀
                        finish: {ਊ਀
                            cilinder: { active: gc('f-cilinder-active'), prep: gv('f-cilinder-prep'), hours: gv('f-cilinder-hours'), rate: gv('f-cilinder-rate') },ਊ਀
                            zgibanje: { active: gc('f-zgibanje-active'), folds: gv('f-zgibanje-folds'), speed: gv('f-zgibanje-speed') },ਊ਀

                            lam: { active: gc('f-lam-active'), type: gv('f-lam-type'), sides: gv('f-lam-sides'), per1000: gv('f-lam-per1000'), prep: gv('f-lam-prep') },ਊ਀
                            delivery: { active: gc('f-delivery-active'), count: gv('f-post-count'), pricePer: gv('f-post-price-per') },ਊ਀
                            deliveryFixed: { active: gc('f-del-fixed-active'), price: gv('f-del-fixed-price') }ਊ਀

                        sheets: (document.getElementById('res-sheets-needed') || {}).innerText || ''ਊ਀
                    }ਊ਀

ਊ਀
                const jsonStr = JSON.stringify(data, null, 2);ਊ਀
                const suggestedName = name.replace(/\s+/g, '_') + '.pola.json';ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                if (window.showSaveFilePicker) {ਊ਀
                    try {ਊ਀
                        const handle = await window.showSaveFilePicker({ਊ਀
                            suggestedName: suggestedName,ਊ਀
                            types: [{ description: 'Pola JSON', accept: { 'application/json': ['.json'] } }],ਊ਀
                        });ਊ਀
                        const writable = await handle.createWritable();ਊ਀
                        await writable.write(jsonStr);ਊ਀
                        await writable.close();ਊ਀
                        return;ਊ਀
                    } catch (e) { alert("Exception in " + "Function" + ": " + e.message + "\n" + e.stack); if (e.name === 'AbortError') return; }ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                const blob = new Blob([jsonStr], { type: 'application/json' });ਊ਀
                const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);ਊ਀
                const a = document.createElement('a');ਊ਀
                a.href = url;ਊ਀
                a.download = suggestedName;ਊ਀
                document.body.appendChild(a); a.click();ਊ਀
                setTimeout(() => { document.body.removeChild(a); window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url); }, 100);ਊ਀
            } catch (err) { alert("Napaka pri shranjevanju: " + err.message); }ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        var g_projectsDirHandle = null;ਊ਀
        var g_diskProjects = [];ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        async function syncWithFolder() {ਊ਀
            try {ਊ਀
                g_projectsDirHandle = await window.showDirectoryPicker();ਊ਀
                await setHandleInIndexedDB(g_projectsDirHandle, 'pola_dir_handle');ਊ਀
                await refreshDiskProjects();ਊ਀
                renderSavedProjects();ਊ਀
                alert("Mapa uspešno povezana in shranjena!");ਊ਀
            } catch (err) {ਊ਀
                if (err.name !== 'AbortError') alert("Napaka: " + err.message);ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        async function refreshDiskProjects() {ਊ਀
            if (!g_projectsDirHandle) return;ਊ਀
            g_diskProjects = [];ਊ਀
            try {ਊ਀
                for await (const entry of g_projectsDirHandle.values()) {ਊ਀
                    if (entry.kind === 'file' && entry.name.endsWith('.json')) {ਊ਀
                        g_diskProjects.push(entry);ਊ਀
                    }ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
            } catch (err) { console.error(err); }ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        async function loadProjectFromDisk(fileName) {ਊ਀
            const entry = g_diskProjects.find(e => e.name === fileName);ਊ਀
            if (!entry) return;ਊ਀
            try {ਊ਀
                const file = await entry.getFile();ਊ਀
                const content = await file.text();ਊ਀
                const data = JSON.parse(content);ਊ਀
                loadProjectData(data);ਊ਀
                alert("Datoteka '" + fileName + "' naložena!");ਊ਀
            } catch (err) { alert("Napaka: " + err.message); }ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        function handleFileSelect(event) {ਊ਀
            const file = event.target.files[0];ਊ਀
            if (!file) return;ਊ਀
            const reader = new FileReader();ਊ਀
            reader.onload = function (e) {ਊ਀
                try {ਊ਀
                    const data = JSON.parse(e.target.result);ਊ਀
                    loadProjectData(data);ਊ਀
                    alert("Projekt uspešno uvožen!");ਊ਀
                } catch (err) { alert("Napaka pri branju datoteke: " + err.message); }ਊ਀
            };ਊ਀
            reader.readAsText(file);ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀

        ਊ਀
        function setHandleInIndexedDB(handle, key) {ਊ਀
            return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {ਊ਀
                const request = indexedDB.open('KalkulatorDB', 1);ਊ਀
                request.onupgradeneeded = e => {ਊ਀
                    e.target.result.createObjectStore('handles');ਊ਀
                };ਊ਀
                request.onsuccess = e => {ਊ਀
                    const db = e.target.result;ਊ਀
                    const tx = db.transaction('handles', 'readwrite');ਊ਀
                    const store = tx.objectStore('handles');ਊ਀
                    store.put(handle, key);ਊ਀
                    tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();ਊ਀
                    tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error);ਊ਀
                };ਊ਀
                request.onerror = e => reject(e.target.error);ਊ਀
            });ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        function getHandleFromIndexedDB(key) {ਊ਀
            return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {ਊ਀
                const request = indexedDB.open('KalkulatorDB', 1);ਊ਀
                request.onupgradeneeded = e => {ਊ਀
                    e.target.result.createObjectStore('handles');ਊ਀
                };ਊ਀
                request.onsuccess = e => {ਊ਀
                    const db = e.target.result;ਊ਀
                    if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('handles')) return resolve(null);ਊ਀
                    const tx = db.transaction('handles', 'readonly');ਊ਀
                    const store = tx.objectStore('handles');ਊ਀
                    const getReq = store.get(key);ਊ਀
                    getReq.onsuccess = () => resolve(getReq.result);ਊ਀
                    getReq.onerror = () => reject(getReq.error);ਊ਀
                };ਊ਀
                request.onerror = e => reject(e.target.error);ਊ਀
            });ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        async function toggleProjectsDropdown() {ਊ਀
            const list = document.getElementById('projects-dropdown-list');ਊ਀
            if (list.style.display === 'block') {ਊ਀
                list.style.display = 'none';ਊ਀
            } else {ਊ਀
                if (!g_projectsDirHandle) {ਊ਀
                    try {ਊ਀
                        const handle = await getHandleFromIndexedDB('pola_dir_handle');ਊ਀
                        if (handle) {ਊ਀
                            if ((await handle.queryPermission({ mode: 'readwrite' })) === 'granted') {ਊ਀
                                g_projectsDirHandle = handle;ਊ਀
                            } else if ((await handle.requestPermission({ mode: 'readwrite' })) === 'granted') {ਊ਀
                                g_projectsDirHandle = handle;ਊ਀
                            }ਊ਀
                        }ਊ਀
                    } catch (e) { alert("Exception in " + "Function" + ": " + e.message + "\n" + e.stack); console.error("IndexedDB error:", e); }ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                if (g_projectsDirHandle) await refreshDiskProjects();ਊ਀
                renderSavedProjects();ਊ਀
                list.style.display = 'block';ਊ਀

ਊ                挀漀渀猀琀 挀氀椀猀琀 㴀 搀漀挀甀洀攀渀琀⸀最攀琀䔀氀攀洀攀渀琀䈀礀䤀搀⠀✀挀甀猀琀ⴀ搀爀漀瀀搀漀眀渀ⴀ氀椀猀琀✀⤀㬀਀

                    }ਊ਀
                };ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                const jsonStr = JSON.stringify(data, null, 2);ਊ਀
                const suggestedName = name.replace(/\s+/g, '_') + '.tiskovna-pola-kalkulator.json';ਊ਀
                if (window.showSaveFilePicker) {ਊ਀
                    try {ਊ਀
                        const handle = await window.showSaveFilePicker({ਊ਀
                            suggestedName: suggestedName,ਊ਀
                            types: [{ description: 'Tiskovna Pola JSON', accept: { 'application/json': ['.json'] } }],ਊ਀
                        });ਊ਀
                        const writable = await handle.createWritable();ਊ਀
                        await writable.write(jsonStr);ਊ਀
                        await writable.close();ਊ਀
                        alert("Datoteka uspešno shranjena!");ਊ਀
                        return;ਊ਀
                    } catch (e) { alert("Exception in " + "Function" + ": " + e.message + "\n" + e.stack); if (e.name === 'AbortError') return; }ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
                const blob = new Blob([jsonStr], { type: 'application/json' });ਊ਀
                const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);ਊ਀
                const a = document.createElement('a');ਊ਀
                a.href = url;ਊ਀
                a.download = suggestedName;ਊ਀
                document.body.appendChild(a); a.click();ਊ਀
                setTimeout(() => { document.body.removeChild(a); window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url); }, 100);ਊ਀
            } catch (err) { alert("Napaka pri shranjevanju: " + err.message); }ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀

        function getWorkOrderHTML() {ਊ਀
            const qtyStr = document.getElementById('quantity').value || '0';ਊ਀
            const qtyArr = qtyStr.split(',').map(x => parseFloat(x.trim().replace(/\./g, ''))).filter(x => !isNaN(x) && x > 0);ਊ਀
            const q = qtyArr[0] || 0;ਊ਀
            const oqStr = document.getElementById('ordered-quantity') ? document.getElementById('ordered-quantity').value : '';ਊ਀
            const oqArr = oqStr.split(',').map(x => parseFloat(x.trim().replace(/\./g, ''))).filter(x => !isNaN(x) && x > 0);ਊ਀
            const oqVal = oqArr[0] || q;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            let calcRes = calculateForSingleQty(q, undefined, undefined, undefined, oqVal);ਊ਀
            if (!calcRes) return "Napaka pri generiranju naloga.";ਊ਀
            let d = calcRes.details;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            let customer = document.getElementById('calc-customer').value || "Ni vpisano";ਊ਀
            let customerCode = document.getElementById('calc-customer-code') ? document.getElementById('calc-customer-code').value : "";ਊ਀
            let custAddress = document.getElementById('calc-cust-address').value || "";ਊ਀
            let custEmail = document.getElementById('calc-cust-email') ? document.getElementById('calc-cust-email').value : "";ਊ਀
            let deliveryAddress = document.getElementById('calc-delivery-address').value || "";ਊ਀
            let contact = document.getElementById('calc-customer-contact') ? document.getElementById('calc-customer-contact').value : "";ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            let dnNum = document.getElementById('calc-dn-number') ? document.getElementById('calc-dn-number').value : "";ਊ਀
            let dnOld = document.getElementById('calc-dn-old') ? document.getElementById('calc-dn-old').value : "";ਊ਀
            let deadline = document.getElementById('calc-dn-deadline') ? document.getElementById('calc-dn-deadline').value : "";ਊ਀
            let urgent = document.getElementById('calc-dn-urgent') && document.getElementById('calc-dn-urgent').checked;ਊ਀
            let packaging = document.getElementById('calc-dn-packaging') ? document.getElementById('calc-dn-packaging').value : "";ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            let quoteNum = document.getElementById('calc-quote-number').value || "";ਊ਀
            let product = document.getElementById('calc-project-name').value || "Ni vpisano";ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            let qty = calcRes.qty || q || 0;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            let materialDesc = document.getElementById('calc-material-desc').value || "";ਊ਀
            let paperType = document.getElementById('calc-paper-type').value || "";ਊ਀
            let paperWeight = document.getElementById('calc-paper-weight').value || "";ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            let date = new Date().toLocaleDateString('sl-SI');ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Formatiranje barvਊ਀
            let front = parseInt(document.getElementById('calc-color-front').value) || 0;ਊ਀
            let back = parseInt(document.getElementById('calc-color-back').value) || 0;ਊ਀
            let isObrat = document.getElementById('calc-is-obrat').checked;ਊ਀
            let colors = front + '/' + (isObrat ? 'OB' : back);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Razrez detailsਊ਀
            let sourceW = d.paper.sourceW || 0;ਊ਀
            let sourceH = d.paper.sourceH || 0;ਊ਀
            let resSizeTxt = document.getElementById('res-size') ? document.getElementById('res-size').innerText.split('x') : [];ਊ਀
            let sheetW = resSizeTxt.length === 2 ? parseFloat(resSizeTxt[0].trim()) : 0;ਊ਀
            let sheetH = resSizeTxt.length === 2 ? parseFloat(resSizeTxt[1].trim()) : 0;ਊ਀
            let sourceYield = d.paper.sourceYield || 1;ਊ਀
            let sourceSheets = d.paper.sourceSheets || 0;ਊ਀
            let totalSheets = d.paper.totalSheets || 0;ਊ਀
            let baseWaste = d.paper.paperWasteSheets || 0;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            let itemsPerSheet = document.getElementById('items-per-sheet').value || "1";ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Dodelava listaਊ਀
            let formatW = document.getElementById('width').value || 0;ਊ਀
            let formatH = document.getElementById('height').value || 0;ਊ਀
            let finishList = getActiveFinishingList();ਊ਀
            let physicalWasteSheets = totalSheets - calcRes.sheetsNeeded;ਊ਀
            let givenDodatekInput = document.getElementById('calc-given-sheets') ? parseInt(document.getElementById('calc-given-sheets').value) : NaN;ਊ਀
            let finalDodatek = physicalWasteSheets;ਊ਀
            let finalTotalSheets = totalSheets;ਊ਀
            let finalSourceSheets = sourceSheets;ਊ਀
            ਊ਀
            if (!isNaN(givenDodatekInput) && givenDodatekInput >= 0) {ਊ਀
                finalDodatek = givenDodatekInput;ਊ਀
                finalTotalSheets = calcRes.sheetsNeeded + givenDodatekInput;ਊ਀
                finalSourceSheets = Math.ceil(finalTotalSheets / sourceYield);ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            ਊ਀

                    sheetW = g_lastSheetW;ਊ਀
                    sheetH = g_lastSheetH;ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
                if (!sheetW) {ਊ਀
                    // Fallback: uporabi profil stroja ali B2ਊ਀
                    const mType = document.getElementById('calc-machine-type').value;ਊ਀
                    const prof = machineProfiles[mType];ਊ਀
                    if (prof && prof.defaultFormat) {ਊ਀
                        const s = sheets.find(sh => sh.name === prof.defaultFormat);ਊ਀
                        if (s) { sheetW = s.w; sheetH = s.h; }ਊ਀
                    }ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
                if (!sheetW) {ਊ਀
                    sheetW = sheets[0].w;ਊ਀
                    sheetH = sheets[0].h;ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
                const gripper = parseFloat(document.getElementById('gripper').value) || 10;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                console.log('[ManualEditor] Pred-populacija:', { w, h, b, itemW, itemH, sheetW, sheetH, gripper, fMode });ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                if (itemW > 0 && itemH > 0) {ਊ਀
                    const srcLayout = optimizeLayout(sheetW, sheetH, itemW, itemH, gripper, fMode);ਊ਀
                    console.log('[ManualEditor] optimizeLayout rezultat:', srcLayout);ਊ਀
                    if (srcLayout && srcLayout.count > 0) {ਊ਀
                        // Prijemalec je zdaj vizualno in logiਊ渁漀 渀愀 搀渀甀 ⠀䠀⤀਀
ਊ                        挀漀渀猀琀 最爀椀瀀伀昀昀猀攀琀圀 㴀 猀爀挀䰀愀礀漀甀琀⸀最爀椀瀀䔀搀最攀 㴀㴀㴀 ✀圀✀ 㼀 最爀椀瀀瀀攀爀 㨀 　㬀਀
ਊ                        挀漀渀猀琀 最爀椀瀀伀昀昀猀攀琀䠀 㴀 　㬀 ⼀⼀ 欀攀爀 樀攀 最爀椀瀀瀀攀爀 瘀攀搀渀漀 猀瀀漀搀愀樀Ⰰ 稀愀਀Ċnemo na vrhu (y=0)ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                        let idCount = Date.now();ਊ਀
                        for (let r = 0; r < srcLayout.rows; r++) {ਊ਀
                            for (let c = 0; c < srcLayout.cols; c++) {ਊ਀
                                m_items.push({ਊ਀
                                    id: idCount++,ਊ਀
                                    x: gripOffsetW + c * srcLayout.itemW,ਊ਀
                                    y: gripOffsetH + r * srcLayout.itemH,ਊ਀
                                    w: srcLayout.itemW,ਊ਀
                                    h: srcLayout.itemH,ਊ਀
                                    bleed: b,ਊ਀
                                    rot: srcLayout.rotਊ਀
                                });ਊ਀
                            }ਊ਀
                        }ਊ਀
                        console.log('[ManualEditor] Dodanih stavkov:', m_items.length);ਊ਀
                    }ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
ਊ਀

ਊ            ⼀⼀ 䤀稀爀愀਀Ċunaj skalo, da se cela pola ujame v 80% oknaਊ਀
            const modal = document.getElementById('manual-editor-modal');ਊ਀
            const canvasArea = modal.querySelector('[data-canvas-area]');ਊ਀
            const areaW = canvasArea ? canvasArea.clientWidth - 80 : window.innerWidth * 0.6;ਊ਀
            const areaH = canvasArea ? canvasArea.clientHeight - 80 : window.innerHeight * 0.75;ਊ਀
            m_scale = Math.max(0.3, Math.min(areaW / sheetW, areaH / sheetH, 1.5));ਊ਀
            console.log('[ManualEditor] setupManualCanvas:', { sheetW, sheetH, areaW, areaH, m_scale });ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Nastavi kontejner na skaliran pxਊ਀
            container.style.width = Math.round(sheetW * m_scale) + 'px';ਊ਀
            container.style.height = Math.round(sheetH * m_scale) + 'px';ਊ਀
            container.style.transform = '';ਊ਀
            container.style.position = 'relative';ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            // Prijemalec (v skaliranih px)ਊ਀
            const grip = document.getElementById('manual-gripper');ਊ਀
            // Prijemalec naj bo vedno spodajਊ਀
            grip.style.top = 'auto';ਊ਀
            grip.style.bottom = '0';ਊ਀
            grip.style.left = '0';ਊ਀
            grip.style.right = '0';ਊ਀
            grip.style.height = Math.round(gripper * m_scale) + 'px';ਊ਀
            grip.style.width = '100%';ਊ਀
            grip.style.borderTop = '2px dashed #f43f5e';ਊ਀
            grip.style.borderBottom = 'none';ਊ਀
            grip.style.borderRight = 'none';ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        function addManualBox() {ਊ਀
            const w = parseFloat(document.getElementById('manual-add-w').value);ਊ਀
            const h = parseFloat(document.getElementById('manual-add-h').value);ਊ਀
            const b = parseFloat(document.getElementById('bleed').value) || 0;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            if (!w || !h) return;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            const id = Date.now();ਊ਀
            m_items.push({ਊ਀
                id: id,ਊ਀
                x: 50, y: 50, // Initial positionਊ਀
                w: w + 2 * b,ਊ਀
                h: h + 2 * b,ਊ਀
                bleed: b,ਊ਀
                rot: falseਊ਀
            });ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            renderManualItems();ਊ਀
            selectManualBox(id);ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        function renderManualItems() {ਊ਀
            const sheet = document.getElementById('manual-sheet');ਊ਀
            sheet.querySelectorAll('.manual-box').forEach(b => b.remove());ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            m_items.forEach(item => {ਊ਀
                const el = document.createElement('div');ਊ਀
                el.className = 'manual-box' + (m_selectedId === item.id ? ' selected' : '');ਊ਀
                // Vse koordinate in dimenzije skaliramo v pxਊ਀
                const px = Math.round(item.x * m_scale);ਊ਀
                const py = Math.round(item.y * m_scale);ਊ਀
                const pw = Math.round(item.w * m_scale);ਊ਀
                const ph = Math.round(item.h * m_scale);ਊ਀
                el.style.width = pw + 'px';ਊ਀
                el.style.height = ph + 'px';ਊ਀
                el.style.left = px + 'px';ਊ਀
                el.style.top = py + 'px';ਊ਀
                el.style.boxSizing = 'border-box';ਊ਀
                el.style.overflow = 'hidden';ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                // Besedilo z dimenzijami stavkaਊ਀
                const netW = Math.round(item.w - 2 * item.bleed);ਊ਀
                const netH = Math.round(item.h - 2 * item.bleed);ਊ਀
                const label = document.createElement('span');ਊ਀
                label.textContent = `${netW}x${netH}`;ਊ਀
                label.style.fontSize = Math.max(9, Math.round(10 * m_scale)) + 'px';ਊ਀
                label.style.pointerEvents = 'none';ਊ਀
                el.appendChild(label);ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                // Pogled na prelivanje (bleed)ਊ਀
                if (item.bleed > 0) {ਊ਀
                    const bleedBox = document.createElement('div');ਊ਀
                    bleedBox.style.position = 'absolute';ਊ਀
                    bleedBox.style.top = Math.round(item.bleed * m_scale) + 'px';ਊ਀
                    bleedBox.style.left = Math.round(item.bleed * m_scale) + 'px';ਊ਀
                    bleedBox.style.width = Math.round((item.w - 2 * item.bleed) * m_scale) + 'px';ਊ਀
                    bleedBox.style.height = Math.round((item.h - 2 * item.bleed) * m_scale) + 'px';ਊ਀
                    bleedBox.style.border = '1px dotted rgba(255,255,255,0.5)';ਊ਀
                    bleedBox.style.pointerEvents = 'none';ਊ਀
                    el.appendChild(bleedBox);ਊ਀
                }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                el.onmousedown = (e) => startDrag(e, item.id);ਊ਀
                el.ondblclick = () => rotateSelectedBox();ਊ਀
ਊ਀
                sheet.appendChild(el);ਊ਀
            });ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            document.getElementById('manual-item-count').innerText = m_items.length;ਊ਀
            renderManualList();ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        function renderManualList() {ਊ਀
            const list = document.getElementById('manual-items-list');ਊ਀
            list.innerHTML = m_items.map((it, idx) => `ਊ਀
                    <div style="padding: 5px; background: rgba(255,255,255,0.05); border-radius: 4px; display: flex; justify-content: space-between;">ਊ਀
                        <span>${idx + 1}. ${it.w - 2 * it.bleed} x ${it.h - 2 * it.bleed} mm</span>ਊ਀
                        <span style="color: #60a5fa;">${it.rot ? 'R' : ''}</span>ਊ਀
                    </div>ਊ਀
                `).join('');ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        function selectManualBox(id) {ਊ਀
            m_selectedId = id;ਊ਀
            renderManualItems();ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        function rotateSelectedBox() {ਊ਀
            if (!m_selectedId) return;ਊ਀
            const it = m_items.find(i => i.id === m_selectedId);ਊ਀
            if (it) {ਊ਀
                const oldW = it.w;ਊ਀
                it.w = it.h;ਊ਀
                it.h = oldW;ਊ਀
                it.rot = !it.rot;ਊ਀
                renderManualItems();ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        function removeSelectedBox() {ਊ਀
            if (!m_selectedId) return;ਊ਀
            m_items = m_items.filter(i => i.id !== m_selectedId);ਊ਀
            m_selectedId = null;ਊ਀
            renderManualItems();ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀

        function applyManualLayout() {ਊ਀
            if (m_items.length === 0) {ਊ਀
                g_manualLayout = null;ਊ਀
                document.getElementById('items-per-sheet').value = 0; // Reset manual overrideਊ਀
            } else {ਊ਀
                g_manualLayout = {ਊ਀
                    items: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(m_items)),ਊ਀
                    sheetW: g_lastSheetW,ਊ਀
                    sheetH: g_lastSheetHਊ਀
                };ਊ਀
                // Update main count inputਊ਀
                document.getElementById('items-per-sheet').value = m_items.length;ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
            closeManualEditor();ਊ਀
            calculate(); // Recalculate and visibly reset canvas layout if clearedਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        // Keyboard shortcuts for editorਊ਀
        window.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {ਊ਀
            const modal = document.getElementById('manual-editor-modal');ਊ਀
            if (modal.style.display !== 'flex') return;ਊ਀
ਊ਀
            if (e.key === 'r' || e.key === 'R') rotateSelectedBox();ਊ਀
            if (e.key === 'Delete' || e.key === 'Backspace') {ਊ਀
                if (document.activeElement.tagName !== 'INPUT') removeSelectedBox();ਊ਀
            }ਊ਀
            if (e.key === 'Enter') applyManualLayout();ਊ਀
            if (e.key === 'Escape') closeManualEditor();ਊ਀
        });ਊ਀
ਊ਀
        // FINAL AUTO-RUNਊ਀
        try {ਊ਀
            applyMachineDefaults();ਊ਀
            renderSavedProjects();ਊ਀
            updateCustomerDatalist();ਊ਀
            renderBasket();ਊ਀
            setTimeout(calculate, 200); // Give it a moment to stabilizeਊ਀
        } catch (e) {ਊ਀
            alert("Exception in " + "Function" + ": " + e.message + "\n" + e.stack);ਊ਀
            console.error("Startup failed:", e);ਊ਀
        }ਊ਀
    </script>ਊ਀
